# Completeness matrix (refresh 2)

This file is the receipt. It does not change app behavior. One row per source requirement. The same rows are in `completeness-matrix.csv`. Open rows are grouped into work packages in `completeness-missing.md`.

## What changed since handover 25

The first matrix (branch `docs/completeness` `65372a687`) measured app `cf2884760`. This refresh measures app `origin/ui/main` `e3264af9f` and backend hengyin `integration/backend-app-r2` `067da9748`. Batches 5–6, the fix batches and every be-gap app branch (`ui/cur-ext-games`, `ui/cur-be-gap-im`, `-club`, `-rewards`, `-workshop`, `-play`, `-merchant`) are merged into that app commit, so no row is IN-PROGRESS H18–H24 any more. The 17 `app.json` pages that were missing from `docs/parity/page-map.csv` are added there in this commit.

## Sources read

- App `origin/ui/main` `e3264af9fc694cfadea419e59d31c790aa8163c1`. Evidence of DONE is a file:line on that commit.
- App route graph: capgraph `app-uimain-e3264af9f.json` (1289 client paths, each with file:line call sites) from `work:/data/workspace/capgraph/out`. Every cited site was re-read on the local tree; sites that are only entries in an admission table (`ChinaReviewedRouteCapability.swift`, `*Lease*.swift`, allowlists, `BusinessRuntimeConfiguration.swift`) do not count as a call site.
- Backend: hengyin `integration/backend-app-r2` `067da9748` (docs on top of code commit `0185418cf`, `git diff --stat` shows docs only). Routes come from capgraph `backend-r6-0185418cf.json` (2305 routes, 319 controllers, 0 unresolved). capgraph `true-missing-r6.json`: 1172 app-called paths, 1109 served, 56 prefix-served, 7 prefix-partial, 0 missing. TEST 8120 runs that backend on `cy_e2e_r2`; `be-int/r6/smoke-all.out` ends `ALLDONE`.
- Owner pack `origin/ui/owner-handover` `15a7ddad1aa7629bcbab3d49d28bf29c9c5c0ee2` (unchanged; see `docs/owner-handover/SUPPLEMENT.md`).
- Mini-program `liboyang42-cpu/hengyin` `chengyinhub-xcx` master `1ca6d3215beee96afdcdfd300dd9176f2d0ac5b3` (unchanged).
- E2E report `docs/release/e2e/E2E-20261113b.md` on the app commit (used by `workflows.md`).

## Status rules

- **DONE** needs evidence on `e3264af9f`: a file:line, plus a served hengyin route or a test.
  - API row: a client call site (capgraph site re-read locally, a composed path such as `"api/coop/" + suffix`, or an enum raw value joined to a path prefix) **and** a route in `067da9748` (exact, `{}` template, `/**` wildcard, or a prefix family whose child is called and served). The capgraph production grant is recorded in the evidence (`grant=yes|gate-only|no:gate(...)`); DONE does not mean "open in China production".
  - PAGE row: the screen file:line exists, page-map status is `exists`, `merged-into:` or `dropped:`, and every API the page calls is DONE (`/api/getwxbindphone` is WeChat-only and is not counted against a page).
  - OWN/APP row: native code that traces the requirement (its id, or a named type for section rows) **and** a test that cites it, or its routes all DONE. §12 rows are bans: DONE means a guard plus a test, or a reproducible absence check (`grep -E <tokens> App Core` = 0 hits). Roll-up rows (十, 十三, 十六, 24.1 …) are DONE when every native child is DONE; back-office rows and owner decisions are excluded from roll-ups and listed separately. **Rule CL-02.R (PROPOSED, SUPPLEMENT.md refresh 3 §WP-R; owner may override):** for a roll-up, a child that is `IN-PROGRESS (production off)` counts as satisfied, because its call site and route exist and only `forcedOff` / `ReleaseMoneyWriteSwitch` keeps it off on purpose; a child that is `screen/code on …, not finished` or MISSING does not count. Nested roll-ups (22.2 → 22.1, 十一 / 十四 → 十三 → 十三-NN) are evaluated to a fixed point by `tools/completeness_rollups.py` (`--apply` closes satisfied roll-ups in this file and the CSV; it never downgrades a row).
- **IN-PROGRESS** has two meanings, written in full in the status column:
  - `production off`: the call site and route exist, and China production `forcedOff` / `ReleaseMoneyWriteSwitch` keeps it off on purpose (money, payout, withdrawal, virtual goods).
  - `screen/code on e3264af9f, not finished`: a screen or code exists, but page-map still says partial/gated or a route is not wired, and the row had no H18–H24 owner.
- **MISSING**: no evidence as above. Rows that were IN-PROGRESS H18–H24 resolve only to DONE or MISSING (the coordinator rule for this refresh), so an ex-H row with a screen but an unwired route is MISSING and its evidence says what is open. One exception: `OWN-READ-工坊付费安装-渠道支付-视频现金兑付` (was H22) stays IN-PROGRESS (production off), because the workshop paid install and video cash payout are wired but kept off by the money gate on purpose.
- Every evidence cell ends with `Was: <status on cf2884760>`.

Rows that were DONE and are not DONE now (17) are findings, not noise: API rows the first matrix counted from an admission-table entry with no client call site, pages that call one of those routes, and owner rows whose old "a test cites the id" match was a coincidence. They are: `PAGE-pages/activity/baoming/baoming`, `PAGE-pages/club/settlement/index`, `PAGE-pages/coop/invite-detail/index`, `PAGE-pages/coop/invite/index`, `PAGE-pages/merchant/team/index`, `PAGE-pages/mylike/mylike`, `PAGE-pages/square/detail/index`, `PAGE-pages/talent/list/index`, `PAGE-subpackageMember/coupon/coupon`, `API-/api/club/lead/unlock-chapter-receipt`, `API-/api/coop/invite/read`, `API-/api/coop/invite/request`, `API-/api/coop/invite/requests`, `API-/api/coop/supply-stations`, `OWN-17.4`, `OWN-21.1`, `OWN-G`.
